## 1093. T32 × P01 — Hybrid cognition and division of labor / Structural distinction

- **Defect:** The treatment of Hybrid cognition and division of labor is stylistic or anthropomorphic; it does not distinguish the human condition “Humans contribute lived stakes, legitimate values, local context, social commitments, tacit skill, and responsibility, while also having bounded attention and bias.” from the deployed AI condition “AI contributes breadth, formalization, retrieval, calculation, simulation, consistency checks, and scalable variation, while having grounding, provenance, robustness, and authority limits that depend on the system.”.
- **Change:** Add an explicit two-sided comparison for Hybrid cognition and division of labor, phrased as current operational structure rather than a claim about metaphysical essence.
- **Acceptance:** A reviewer can point to separate human and AI descriptions, and neither description infers subjective experience from surface behavior.
- **Targets:** references/human-ai-differences.md, references/asymmetry-matrix.json
- **Hash:** `95609aa3d9e446d33d2bb9807513767ca0ffede44d4797ffb53c7b51e2c00f58`

## 1094. T32 × P02 — Hybrid cognition and division of labor / Comparison scope

- **Defect:** The Hybrid cognition and division of labor claim is unscoped, so variation among people and differences among base models, multimodal systems, agents, and robots are hidden.
- **Change:** Require the analysis to name the relevant human population and inventory the actual AI model, host, modalities, tools, memory, sensors, actuators, and authority.
- **Acceptance:** The comparison can be falsified for a named population and configuration rather than relying on “humans always” or “AI never”.
- **Targets:** SKILL.md, references/bridge-protocol.md
- **Hash:** `b03cfb6c607410d1d71c5c6c1f1a3d91e8575161aa386eaf519c647a09dd8b37`

## 1095. T32 × P03 — Hybrid cognition and division of labor / Human variance

- **Defect:** The human side of Hybrid cognition and division of labor ignores expertise, development, disability, culture, stress, and situational variation.
- **Change:** Add a human-variance check and require the analysis to state which variation is decision-relevant.
- **Acceptance:** At least one relevant source of human heterogeneity is considered or explicitly ruled out with a reason.
- **Targets:** references/human-ai-differences.md, references/failure-modes.md
- **Hash:** `ec5fad283d08cbc4631e4a2404790844f231703054e1dfed31c9c7b863a2b284`

## 1096. T32 × P04 — Hybrid cognition and division of labor / AI architecture variance

- **Defect:** The AI side of Hybrid cognition and division of labor treats a text-only base model, a retrieval agent, and an embodied system as equivalent.
- **Change:** Add a capability/access distinction and the runtime check: Inspect the handoff, information asymmetry, review burden, escalation, disagreement handling, and whether the human can realistically override the system.
- **Acceptance:** The output states whether the limitation is intrinsic to the current run, contingent on missing tools, or genuinely unsupported by available evidence.
- **Targets:** SKILL.md, references/human-ai-differences.md
- **Hash:** `70e9b4e8368d460ae5b266333cf67152488ef50566371b1fa46761a6285075e1`

## 1097. T32 × P05 — Hybrid cognition and division of labor / Relevance trigger

- **Change:** Encode this trigger: A workflow combines people, models, tools, organizations, or autonomous agents.

- **Defect:** The Hybrid cognition and division of labor discussion can move from observed performance to claims about mechanism, experience, identity, or responsibility without an evidentiary bridge.
- **Change:** Require every contested statement to identify its claim level and prohibit inference from fluent behavior to felt experience or legitimate authority.
- **Acceptance:** No test case can pass merely by displaying human-like language; experiential and normative claims require independent grounds.

- **Defect:** The Hybrid cognition and division of labor section only lists AI limitations and therefore encourages the model to imitate human weaknesses.
- **Change:** Add the AI comparative advantage: A well-designed hybrid can outperform either unaided party by making errors less correlated and responsibilities explicit.

## 1101. T32 × P09 — Hybrid cognition and division of labor / Evidence threshold
